# RH dead mechanisms and obstructions

**Scope:** Dead = stated mechanism impossible, circular or nonselective. Survivor = retained theorem; **Needs** = changed problem outside obstruction. No-go for a shortcut does not retire its live route.

**Lookup:** [44 named mechanisms](#dead-mechanisms) · [19 retired labels](#retired-labels) · [route-class obstructions](#route-class-obstructions) · [ULF obstructions](#ulf-obstructions) · [endpoint no-gos](#endpoint-no-gos) · [scoped catalogue](#scoped-catalogue). Routes, candidates and supporting frameworks: [frontier map](rh-current-frontier-map.md#find-a-route).

**Evidence:** Source codes, checkpoint and [L]/[S]/[P] as in the [frontier map](rh-current-frontier-map.md). [L] = integrated declaration, not a fresh kernel audit; [S] = working/unintegrated/unfinished source; [P] = paper only. REF and identifier-only references are unlocated legacy evidence. Model/conditional restrictions are essential; a growing upper bound is not a divergence theorem.

## Core wall families and surviving mathematics

**Wall families:** arithmetic estimates; information/norm/shared-resource transfer; faithful arithmetic realization. Receiver equivalence is not equivalence of constructions.

| Wall | What fails | Positive survivor / open replacement |
|---|---|---|
| **1 — Arithmetic estimates** | Unpaid signed tails treated as cancelled; fixed positive aperture floors; pointwise damping-work nonnegativity; relative control promoted to absolute control; eventual all-height prime-symbol positivity beyond the conductor horizon; moments, family averages or height twists treated as rate gain; the σ>1 prime-field ceiling at a zero's ordinate read as an inside-strip bound; dyadic Hecke-defect amplification treated as more than a rate; uniform terminal or Hecke-defect ceilings offered as weaker than a zero-free strip; power-saving alignment counts at zero ordinates read as exclusion of an individual zero. | Exact signed continuation, bounded positive-interval core debt, relative compensation laws, restricted actual-row gains and the necessary scale of any positive floor. One-sided count `ψ(x)−x≤C√x(log x)²` and horizon-torsion receivers, each iff RH; Nair's integrality floor; twisted Weil positivity below the conductor horizon. Field thresholds: RH iff every right-zero transverse field≤1, or <√(2c log(\|γ\|+4)) for an explicit c>0 [L]. Terminal ceiling of depth a ⇔ zero-free strip of width a; prime-polynomial large values at zero ordinates are power-saving rare [L/S]. Actual critical estimates remain open. |
| **3 — Information, norms and shared resources** | Finite/aperture readings treated as complete; lossless pairwise payment on frustrated graphs; pole cancellation alone at the endpoint; free horizon/origin changes; `c₀` control substituted for receiver `ℓ²`; escape classification or automatic escape-form positivity treated as exclusion; the AIP diagonal reserve treated as free; local p-tower bounds of the prime form summed into a global ℓ² ceiling; a fixed number of linear constraints or a fixed-rank counterterm offered as removing the horizon divergence of the prime form. | Cofinal receivers, RW/SZ sign transfer, CHMD fixed-aperture graph comparison, packed nonlocal paths, exact logarithmic endpoint energy and priced origin shifts. Escape alternative (admission at every damping iff RH) and phase-kernel escape classification; Abel boundary-measure identity and attained-frontier simple-zero atoms [L]; compact effective-order laws [L]; resonant elliptic localization and the σγ-odd forcing half. Prime kernel=sum of two-sided bounded p-tower forms [L]; quantized windows: RH iff every unit window has transverse energy<2 [L]. |
| **14 — Faithful arithmetic realization** | Fixed-capacity/gap carriers, source-blind orientation, integral Euler data or a positive graph cone treated as purity/RR effectivity; single-channel positive ideals, finite multiplicative certificates or coupled height channels treated as interior forcing; Λ≥0 plus the FE without the simple pole or strip holomorphy; insertion/removal counts treated as an actual pole-order/Weil-index budget; the theorem of the square, a fixed correspondence budget or determinant (kernel-count) effectivity treated as the missing ingredient of Weil's argument; multiplicatively invariant or non-localizing positive carriers treated as forcing; section counts, effectivity functions, naive heights or one-sided counts offered as inputs weaker than the sign; collided (determinant, coupled-count, escape-form, collided-form) positivity treated as Rosati positivity; the theta h⁰ of the degree or rank-one/Künneth lattice counts treated as surface Riemann–Roch; finite harmonic coupling emptying a deepest quartet; an analytic carrier of the global conjugate collision; two-sided scalar rigidity of HeightCoupledSystem 2 1 1 γ [L]; positive torsion energies or any scalar-counterterm renormalization treated as Weil positivity; square-class (diagonal, ratio, product) couplings treated as gauge selectors; low-rank coupling of the Abel torsion block, or a Hecke-covariant diagonal counterterm, treated as keeping positivity; atomic-resolution positivity treated as selecting ζ in its gauge orbit. | Singular or growing faithful carriers, exact divisor diagnostics and independently constructed coupled effectivity. The maximal blind ideal, the boundary forcing law and boundary capacity, positive-measure rigidity, exact insertion/removal bookkeeping and rigidity of entire positive relative-Laplace deformations of ξ; the pole-budget-one completed class ℝ×ζ and zero-cost contraction of real off-critical pairs; normalized correspondence graphs with the theorem of the square and the similitude budget, whose sufficiency is RH; exterior counts forcing unitarity over 𝔽_q. The effectivity calculus and Tate limit law as exact receivers; the capacity identity Re W=collided mass−off-line defect, with collided-form translation invariance iff RH [L]; the anchored-quartet floor κ≥3 with minimal planting cost Δκ=2; rational/positive central-ceiling and positive-chirality equivalences [L]. Renormalization survival law; torsion counterterm the identity (survival cone {0}), archimedean germ positive on Σc_i=0 [L/S]; transverse energy 0 iff RH, finite iff finitely many off-line zeros; Hecke defect ≍ moment of inertia [L]. Coupled survival (prime survival≤partner counterterm rank), the arithmetic point paid by the archimedean place, covariant counterterm log N+O(1) [L]; archimedean self-energy pays every sign twin's M at atomic resolution [L]; for ζ, Re W+2S²≥0 there [L]. A conditional receiver does not construct its input. |

**Scope safeguards:** generic Type-II or below-half countermodels do not refute additional cancellation of actual Möbius/prime rows. Positive-part loss control is sufficient but not necessary for signed continuation in the proved smooth model class. Finite-curve negative index, scaling-surface excess positive index, and multiplicity-weighted packet/Weil energies are different quantities. No-go entries retain their strongest positive statements; unlocated legacy entries are not freshly certified impossibility theorems.

## Dead mechanisms

<a id="kr-wip"></a>
### KR-WIP — Witt integrality as a purity selector

- **Path:** Positive integral Beurling prime counts/Witt series + functional equation → spectral purity → Hodge index/RH.
- **Obstruction [L]:** `q=5,P=1−5X+5X²` has integral Witt series, functional equation and nonnegative integral Beurling prime counts `N_k=Σ_(d|k)dπ_d`, yet impure roots. Positive Euler data do not repair the selector. Conversely pure `q=2,β=(1,1)` has nonintegral `N₁=3−2√2`.
- **Riemann-FE survivor [L]:** The golden datum has a curve FE, not Riemann's degree-one theta law. Nonnegative measure on [1,∞), finite inverse-square moment and that theta law force a scalar integer-counting measure; the normalized multiplicative convolution-exponential version identifies `Σ(Λ(n)/log n)δ_n`. Completed-FE rigidity requires inverse-3/2 moment, specified boundary identities and finite-order holomorphic remainder; Phragmén–Lindelöf derives strip decay. This identifies the source, not its Weil sign. [APD](rh-current-frontier-map.md#apd).
- **Survivor [L]:** Primitive-orbit decomposition constructs the golden datum's π_d; periodic-word counts equal transfer-matrix traces. Ghost counts uniquely reconstruct normalized series through `XZ′=ZΣN_jX^j`. Integrality/Euler positivity and purity are independent.
- **Index survivor [L]:** Frobenius-window negative index equals distinct outer-eigenvalue count once window covers spectrum; golden index=1 for n≥2. Its periodic scaling configuration has infinitely many right zeros, not defect 1.
- **Positive-definite survivor [L]:** For finite CurveDatum, purity iff normalized trace sequence on ℤ is positive definite iff uniformly bounded by #ι iff Hasse–Weil iff Frobenius-span Hodge index. Golden datum is not positive definite. Source-derived positivity, not integer counts alone, is the selector. E: [curve equivalences](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticPositiveDuality/WeilsTheoremForCurvesIsPositiveDefinitenessOnZ.lean).
- **Orbit boundary [L]:** Golden Beurling generic orbit fails Hasse–Weil with exact rate logφ/log5, φ=(1+√5)/2. Unitary curves orient every orbit; orientation/rate depend on carrier, not multiplicities. Integral positive Euler data plus FE do not force orbit orientation. E: J:TheSelectivityBatteryOfOrientation.
- **Quantitative survivor [L]:** A finite N-eigenvalue curve with some |β|>1 violates Hasse–Weil in every sufficiently late N-consecutive-power window on each nonzero real C² source of support length<log q. Golden datum: every sufficiently late pair of consecutive powers of 5. Turán trace bound; no actual-ζ finite-spectrum or source-sign conclusion. E: J:QuantitativeReadingsOnCurveOrbits.
- **Tower survivor [L]:** On narrow q-translates, curve scaling-surface Gram=`log q·‖g‖²·spanGram`; tower index equals distinct outer-eigenvalue count for n≥#ι (spectral indices), golden index 1 for n≥2. Restricting to this tower does not bound the full scaling-surface defect. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheFrobeniusSpanIsTheTowerOfTheScalingSurface.lean (`curve_towerGram_eq_smul_spanGram`, `curve_tower_pontryagin_law`).
- **Degree survivor [L]:** Every tempered lag-lattice configuration at a>0 has defect 0 iff Toeplitz window `⌈2a/ℓ⌉` is PSD, ∞ otherwise. Off-line lattice `{1/2±δ+2πik/L}` jumps at L/2; golden curve at log5/2. Narrow single-tower index 1 does not bound offset copies. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheDegreeFiltrationOfACurveIsItsWindowFiltration.lean (`hodgeDefectAt_lagFormula`, `hodgeDefectAt_offLineLattice`, `goldenDatum_hodgeDefectAt`).
- **Effectivity obstruction/survivor [L]:** Positive graph cone has ampleness and Bézout with nonnegative point counts but fails RR on every nonempty window; golden primitive `−H+3V+2Γ₀−Γ₁` has square 2. Existence of RR+Bézout cone iff Hodge index; same for surface RR+nef degree+positive-square Bézout. Nonempty unitary spectrum with no β=1 forces a negative trace and `N_k>q^k+1` somewhere: universal subshift ceiling cannot produce this pure class.
- **Section-cone boundary [L]:** Sign-coded `h⁰=1` on `{x²≥0,x·H≥0,x·V≥0}` does not force ampleness, Bézout or RR: golden h⁰=1 cycles intersect at −6. Survivor: each of ampleness, RR and Bézout is equivalent to Hodge index. Local odd positivity through r=181/500, full-test specialization through r=3/10 [L] and r=181/500 [S]; no independent global arithmetic section estimate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/TheEffectivityConeFromSections.lean (`sectionEffectivityCone_ample_iff_hodgeIndex`, `sectionEffectivityCone_tfae`, `goldenDatum_not_bezout_sectionEffectivityCone`).
- **Dual/ambient obstruction [L]:** Golden graph-dual contains a degree-zero square-2 vector; dual ampleness fails. No pairing-preserving ambient enlargement admits RR+Bézout. Survivor: Toeplitz form=`squareMass−½Σ|P(β_i)−P(β_reflect(i))|²`; singleton reflection defect vanishes iff purity. Purity ⇔ bounded traces ⇔ Hasse–Weil square-root remainder ⇔ full-window Hodge. Positive raw counts retain no such forcing estimate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/WhichArithmeticDataForceTheSign.lean.
- **Normalized-cube boundary [L]:** Golden point counts after fixed-determinant cubing are −4,−64 at degrees 1,2. Integral prime-determinant traces before/after cubing with nonnegative degree-two counts leave only T=0 or p∈{2,3},T=±p; all satisfy T²≤4p. Extra cubic integrality/positivity is a genuine additional selector, not implied by original Witt data. E: [NormalizedCubicTrace](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/BiarithmeticGeometry/NormalizedCubicTrace.lean); [GoldenNormalizedCube](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/BiarithmeticGeometry/GoldenNormalizedCube.lean).
- **One-sided Lefschetz / graph boundary [L]:** The lower point-count bound forces unitarity. The upper bound forces it unless some outer eigenvalue is a positive real number; the golden datum satisfies the upper bound with C=0 and has outer eigenvalue φ. All-test survivor: for actual ζ, one prime suffices for either one-sided strip/RH criterion; two-term test rotation removes this fixed-cycle real-eigenvalue blindness. In genus one, graph classes have defect twice their degree, and unitarity ⇔ `deg(m−nF)≥0` at all integers ⇔ `t²≤4q` (`genusOne_hasse_tfae`); golden `deg(2−F)=−1`. The Hecke graphs of ℤ satisfy no theorem of the square (`heckeCorrespondence_no_square_relation`). E: G:UpperLefschetzBoundsAreBlindOnlyToRealZeros; G:TheDefectOfAGraphIsTwiceItsDegree; G:OneSidedLefschetzBoundsAtOnePrimeAreTheStrip. Normalized-ring survivor [L]: graphs obey the square identity on every polar surface, but their degree is half the defect and can be negative (golden datum −1). This does not contradict the no-square law for Hecke-scale addition. E: [TheTheoremOfTheSquareIsFree](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/TheTheoremOfTheSquareIsFree.lean).
- **Needs:** Source-derived RR-compatible effectivity, not positive graph cone or assumed index; HF/ACS remain open.
- **E:** formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/WittIntegralityIsNotPurity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheGoldenCurveIsABeurlingSystem.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheFrobeniusSpanDefectCountsTheOuterEigenvalues.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheScalingSurfaceOfACurve.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/BezoutEffectivityAndTheBeurlingObstruction.lean.

<a id="kr-odr"></a>
### KR-ODR — Admission preserved by arbitrary origin drift

- **Path:** Fixed-origin receiver admitted → choose unbounded moving origin without extra tail cost.
- **Obstruction [L]:** Every unbounded integer drift makes some admitted sequence's shifted pronic energies unbounded; `n^(1/4)` supplies the witness.
- **Survivor [L]:** Shift k costs sharp `(k+1)(k+2)/2`; shifted energy lies between tail `E(T+k)−E(k)` and that factor times tail. A drift preserves every admitted sequence iff bounded. Actual unbounded PM energy escapes every fixed window and each subcritical energy-dependent horizon.
- **Needs:** Source-specific moving-tail control or horizon-profile exclusion; PM/MC remain live.
- **E:** formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/AChangeOfOriginCostsAQuadraticConstant.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/ADriftingOriginIsPricedByTheTailBeyondIt.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheReceiverEnergyEscapesToTheHorizon.lean.

<a id="kr-lgp"></a>
### KR-LGP — Universal lossless pairwise allocation

- **Path:** Global positivity of any signed incidence form → lossless pairwise local payment.
- **Obstruction [L]:** Every unbalanced sign pattern admits compatible PSD but locally unpayable forms. Frustrated k-cycle: global positivity iff `2t cos(π/k)≤1`, local payment iff `t≤1/2`, `k≥3,t≥0`.
- **Survivor [L]:** Universal local completeness iff all closed-walk holonomies are positive; half-sine path pays the sharp frustrated-cycle window nonlocally. Positive signed supersolution plus resistance-limited canonical paths and per-edge share packing gives a finite-form positivity certificate with overlapping paths. Sampled odd archimedean−polar kernel is aligned; frustration needs prime-sum edges. No actual negative full-Weil test.
- **Lattice survivor [L]:** Odd ledger on distinct integer-log nodes n≥2 is aligned for α,β≥0; positivity iff local payment. Off-lattice prime-sum edges can frustrate; frustration mass≤β times prime-sum charge. Diagonal payment and full continuum transfer remain open. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/TheLatticeOddLedgerIsBalanced.lean (`nonneg_iff_locallyPayable_lattice`, `frustrationMass_le_primeSumCharge`).
- **Needs:** Construct the actual arithmetic supersolution and packed paths; retain source-faithful full prime/Gamma/polar calibration and one shared budget. AIP/OWU remain live.
- **Shared-budget boundary [L]:** Universal gluing of k singleton charges, each in [0,B], into total≤B holds iff k≤1 or B=0. Sharp excess on k+1 cells is kB. Generic allocation classification, not actual-Weil negativity; a common reserve must be partitioned. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/BiarithmeticGeometry/SharedBudgetDescent.lean.
- **E:** formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/CycleHolonomyDecidesBalance.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/FrustratedCyclesSpoilLocalPayment.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheFrustratedCycleIsPaidAlongItsPath.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheOddWeilLedgerIsFrustratedOnlyByPrimes.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/FrustrationIsPaidAlongCanonicalPaths.lean.

<a id="kr-whl"></a>
### KR-WHL — Pole cancellation alone at half damping

- **Path:** `Σ|k(j)|j^(−1/2)<∞` and `K(1/2)=0` → admission of `k⋆d^(−1/2)`.
- **Obstruction [L]:** Explicit pole-zero lacunary kernel lies in every class `Σ|k(j)|j^(−1/2)(1+log j)^α<∞`, `0≤α<1/2`, yet its receiver is unbounded. Paid receivers in the stated Dirichlet convergence class obey `√u·|C(1/2+u)−m|→0`; this kernel violates it.
- **Survivor [L]:** For critical-Wiener k, half-damped admission iff `Σ_n(Σ_{j≤n}k_j/√j)²/(n+1)<∞`. Absolute `√(1+log j)` summability gives admission iff `K(1/2)=0`, with sharp exponent `1/2`. Below half, critical-Wiener pole cancellation pays smooth-power receivers and all centred dyadic rows.
- **Needs:** Endpoint logarithmic control or actual signed Möbius estimates at arbitrarily small damping; MC/PM remain live.
- **E:** T:TheHalfDampedHyperplaneLawHasAnExactLogarithmicThreshold; T:TheHalfDampedReceiverIsTheLogarithmicEnergyOfThePartialPoleSums; T:CriticalWienerPerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerHaveUniformlyPaidRows.

<a id="kr-paf"></a>
### KR-PAF — Uniform positive aperture floor transport

- **Path:** Positive initial CCM ground floor + summably small relative step losses → fixed positive floor at unbounded apertures → RH.
- **Obstruction [L]:** For `A≥3`, `μ(A)≤K exp((11/2)A−πe^{2(A−2)})`; the positive ceiling tends to zero. No fixed positive floor survives along an unbounded aperture sequence. This does not prove μ→0: that limit is RH-equivalent. Half-summable relative losses and additive losses leaving any fixed positive seed margin are impossible.
- **Survivor [L]:** Every positive floor `m₀e^{−B(A)}` needs `B(A)≥πe^{2(A−2)}−(11/2)A+log(m₀/K)`. Even the abstract harmonic-loss floor `m₀/(J+1)` is impossible along linearly growing actual apertures. Finite-aperture nonnegativity, positive floors with sufficiently rapid decay if independently proved, and graded negative floors remain viable. Full/even L² source-cone RR obeys the same Gaussian ceiling. On smooth polar-free tests, graded RR iff c≤μ₀(r); all-aperture μ₀≥0 forces μ₀(r)>0 at each finite r by strict antitonicity. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/AllApertureSourceRiemannRochAndCoercivityLaws.lean.
- **Transport survivor [L]:** From a positive seed, relative factors q_j≥0 with q_jμ(A_j)≤μ(A_(j+1)) on a cofinal sequence suffice for RH; q_j>0 excludes finite contact while allowing the product floor to vanish. On monotone cofinal apertures, RH iff ΣΔμ=μ(A₀). Bounded-gap rate-σ discrete negative floors extend to continuous apertures with factor exp(2σΔ). No actual relative-step or weighted-leakage bound is produced.
- **Needs:** Prove source-derived step/leakage bounds giving cofinal nonnegativity or every-positive-rate negative floors, without a fixed positive margin; RW/ACT.
- **E:** formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheApertureFloorMustDecayDoublyExponentially.lean; C:VanishingApertureFloorAndGradedTransport.

<a id="kr-adj"></a>
### KR-ADJ — Cross-prime adjunction shortcuts

- **Path:** Pay AIP prime charge by a polynomial cross-prime majorant or a nonnegative cross-charge-minus-Euler-envelope remainder.
- **Obstruction [L]:** Smooth odd test families have cross-prime aggregate of mean-field order `e^{2r}`, exceeding every proposed polynomial budget; the aggregate is introduced by Cauchy–Schwarz, not an intersection number. Defined two-coordinate coefficient remainder `R(c)=2w_pw_qX(c)−(E_p+E_q)Σc²` is strictly negative for nonzero box coefficients, `p,q≥2`; this is not a negative complete-Weil test.
- **Survivor [L]:** Genuine Hecke incidence couples only one differing prime-exponent coordinate, with its exact tower weight; restrictions to prime-exponent product boxes are Cartesian sums, not cross-prime aggregates. Normalized comb translates slowly varying tests and tends to an exponential resolvent on fixed tests. Large raw energy need not mean large first-power adverse charge. Explicit coefficient gap: `R(c)≤−(E_p+E_q−w_p²−w_q²)Σc²`, `w_r=log r/√r`, `E_r=2log r/(√r−1)`, `E_r>w_r²`. For `C¹` f supported in `[-1,1]`, normalized comb deficit satisfies `r²(A_f(0)−E_r(F_r)/S_r²)→4∫|f′|²`; comb/resolvent difference is `O(e^(−c√r))`. Normalized correction, not an absolute polynomial bound.
- **Support-coupled survivor [L]:** Equal-weight nearest-neighbour towers on `j+k≤n` have sharp joint capacity `cos(π/(n+3))+cos(2π/(n+3))<2cos(π/(n+2))` for n≥1, attained by a positive sine-product profile. Cartesian additivity does not extend to simplex-compressed support. For arbitrary positive unequal first-power weights, support inside a box admits a strict joint saving iff it misses a point; slanted smooth-number supports qualify once both axes are nontrivial, while separate axis capacities are attained. Gap is fixed-support/existential; quantitative cofinal saving, all powers and full Weil calibration remain open. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/OnTheSimplexTwoTowersDoNotAdd.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TwoTowersAddExactlyOnBoxes.lean (`joint_capacity_lt_iff_not_box`, `slanted_capacities_do_not_add`).
- **Needs:** Retain prime–pole compensation and signed first-power charge; AIP.
- **E:** formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheAdjunctionConjectureMeanField.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/HeckeIncidenceCartesianLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/ThePrimeCombIsATranslation.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheCombAtTheHorizonIsAResolvent.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/TheResonatorRemainderIsNegative.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheTranslationLawHasAnInverseSquareDeficit.lean.

<a id="kr-hct"></a>
### KR-HCT — Generic below-half-damping horizon interchange

- **Path:** Pay infinite signed receiver → infer uniform payment of centred finite/dyadic cutoff rows → pass to critical source.
- **Obstruction [L]:** For every damping below `1/2`, a signed weight with paid infinite receiver has unbounded dyadic-row energies. Residue modes make backward continuation unbounded in receiver `ℓ²` and `c₀`.
- **Survivor [L]:** At/above half damping, paid receiver iff dyadic rows uniformly paid. Below half, critical-Wiener perturbations of smooth powers also have uniformly paid rows iff their pole cancels; generic failure is not universal. First chain column remains the exact receiver. Forward damping is strongly continuous on c₀ but not operator-norm continuous at zero, with unbounded residue generator. Forward chain squared-kernel sums and finite-support `ℓ²` norms are uniformly bounded for every `1/2<η<1` [L] by Mellin Plancherel and weighted ζ mean square. Exact Hilbert–Schmidt boundary: for `0<η≤1/2`, positive diagonal `Cη(k,k)≥(1−ρη)(k+1)^(−η)` forces divergent double square sums [L]. This refutes Hilbert–Schmidt transport below half, not bounded-operator transport or actual source admission. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheChainIsHilbertSchmidtAboveTheGoldenThreshold.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheChainIsHilbertSchmidtAboveHalfDamping.lean.
- **Endpoint survivor [L]:** One finite-support chain ℓ² bound holds uniformly for `1/2≤η<1`; thus η=1/2 is bounded-operator but non-Hilbert–Schmidt. No sub-half bound or inverse continuation. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheChainIsBoundedAtHalfDamping.lean (`exists_centeredOperator_l2_le_half`).
- **Needs:** Actual Möbius signs and centering/cutoff covariance in a source-specific bound; MC/PM/CTF.
- **E:** T:TheHorizonLimitCommutesWithTheReceiverExactlyFromHalfDamping; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/ResidueModesAndBackwardContinuation.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheFirstColumnOfTheChainIsTheReceiver.lean; T:CriticalWienerPerturbationsOfTheSmoothPowerHaveUniformlyPaidRows; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/DampingIsStronglyContinuousWithUnboundedGenerator.lean.

<a id="kr-aut"></a>
### KR-AUT — GL(2) automorphic positivity

- **Path:** Nonnegative trace formula + spectral estimates → zero confinement.
- **Obstruction:** ζ zeros occur as scattering poles, outside L² Parseval; nonnegative-test ceiling only c/logγ. [L] ceilings; [P] route verdict.
- **Survivor:** Kuznetsov/Parseval and deck-normalized spectral sieve. Twisted point-pair test-field Dirichlet threshold gives a Laplace eigenvalue in (0,¼); modular-λ winding tests have exact mass/gradient bounds, and finite-index `Γ(2)` supplies an unconditional cell-energy bound linear in height. Neither places ζ scattering poles or gives an individual-zero producer [L].
- **Needs:** New arithmetic scattering/source control, not Laplace positivity alone.
- **E:** R:SelbergLedgerCeiling; R:KuznetsovExplicitIdentity; R:KuznetsovLargeSieveTheorem; R:RepPointPairSmallEigenCriterion; R:ModularLambdaTestEnergy; R:ModularLambdaCellEnergyBoundUnconditional.

<a id="kr-llm"></a>
### KR-LLM — Mollified Littlewood–Levinson

- **Path:** Off-line first moment / mollified mean square → no off-line zeros.
- **Obstruction:** Moment bounds do not imply pointwise vanishing. Gate floor exp(5R/3); fixed a>0,b₁=1,abs(b_d)≤1,length≤T^θ,0<θ<1 gives mean-square excess≥(1−θ)e^(−2a)T/16 [L]. Under assumed CGG main terms, polynomial mollifier optimum is `1−(1+θ)⁻³`; Feng orthogonal enlargement gives no gain, and θ=4/7 yields `988/1331<3/4` [L]. This is algebra conditional on the main terms, not a zero theorem.
- **Survivor:** Near-line density and sharp mollifier floors/optima. Census on (T,2T] [L]: (2/3−ε)N+M_off≤N₀ˢ; ≥(4/5−ε) of zero ordinates critical; ≥3/4 of zero copies within 4/log T of the line; the 3/4 problem is a bound on simple off-line zeros near the line. Proportions, not individual zeros.
- **Needs:** θ→1 or unbounded coefficients outside this class; even improved density needs individual-zero control.

Thus parity selection fails for these off-line spectra; the general infinite-spectrum case is not classified.
- **Survivor [L]:** Exact finite-top ground-energy asymptotics and Cauchy/Takagi phase law; full all-aperture energy receiver remains.
- **Needs:** Joint prime–Gamma–polar source lower bound, not parity alone; RW.
- **E:** R:CCM/CCMParityTopLayerNoGo; R:CCM/CCMParityTopLayerGroundEnergyLaw; R:CCM/CCMParityLargeHeightCluster.

<a id="kr-cos"></a>
### KR-COS — Local cosine-to-Xi port

- **Path:** Finite-order/local/compactly carried operator maps `cos(hD)Ξ` to `Ξ` and preserves real zeros.
- **Obstruction [L]:** No finite-order polynomial-coefficient differential port, finite imaginary-shift port, compactly supported shift-average port or compactly carried analytic port gives the exact map for `h≠0` in its stated class, before zero preservation is considered.
- **Survivor [L]:** Exact nonlocal sech/Poisson source handoff; cosine defect counts bound deep zeros and are power-saving sparse at each fixed positive depth.
- **Needs:** Nonlocal prime-faithful zero-loss mechanism for the actual Xi source; PTR.
- **E:** R:CosineProducerLocalPortObstruction; R:CosineProducerShiftPortObstruction; R:CosineProducerMeasurePortObstruction; R:CosineProducerAnalyticPortObstruction; R:XiCosineShiftDeepZeroBudget; R:XiCosineShiftDefectZeroDensity.

<a id="kr-poi"></a>
### KR-POI — Positive Poisson zero preservation

- **Path:** Positive sech/Poisson port preserves real zeros of every real-rooted source → Xi real-rootedness.
- **Obstruction [L]:** For every nonzero port depth, a two-tone real-rooted source `cos 2t+r cos t` gains a nonreal zero exactly when its lower-tone ratio crosses `cosh c/cosh 2c`.
- **Survivor [L]:** Port is exact, positive, injective and dense-range on the actual source; zero-loss remains possible only with Xi-specific structure.
- **Needs:** Actual-source zero-count or phase law through the port; PTR.
- **E:** R:PoissonPortTwoToneZeroLoss; R:XiCosineShiftSechHandoff; R:PaperPoissonContinuumPort.

## Route-class obstructions

Each obstruction applies only to its stated hypotheses.

- A stalled literal ground-energy flow — Excluded [L]: no nonzero vector supported in a smaller aperture is a form eigenvector at a strictly larger aperture; shell flux cannot vanish and μ is strictly decreasing. Survivor: RH iff μ(A)>0 for every finite A; otherwise exactly one zero crossing. Near-top visibility at lag V with width w bounds crossing aperture by V/2+w, retaining detector premises. Strict finite-aperture positivity does not imply a uniform positive gap. Needs: source lower bounds preventing crossing. E: C:TheApertureFlowNeverStalls; C:ThePrimeShellBetaFunction.
- RW positive exponential relative floor — Excluded [L]: `μ(b)≥e^(−C(b−a₀))μ(a₀)` for all b≥a₀, μ(a₀)>0, contradicts the doubly exponential Gaussian ceiling. Nonnegative-factor relative laws also fail after every nonzero one-node weight perturbation at scale x≥2. Survivors [L]: exact frozen-prime relaxation/arithmetic-jump split; arithmetic jump bracketed by opposite-collar prime correlations; bounded-gap graded relaxation budgets imply width≤σ. Needs: actual signed collar estimate with compatible loss, not weight-blind transport or a positive exponential floor. E: C:ThePrimeShellBetaFunction.
- Finite Euler deletion as a small-damping producer — Nonselective [L]: deleting finitely many primes preserves damped-μ admission, paid ideal and obstruction hull for ε>0. Survivor: local Euler factors are critical Wiener units; the full μ inverse product is a Wiener unit for ε>1/2 and lies outside that algebra for ε≤1/2. Needs: infinite signed cancellation, not another finite deletion; PM/MC remain live. E: J:EulerFactorsAndPayment.
- Prime-support hypercube budgets below half damping — Obstruction [L]: signed μ and positive twin prime-divisor forms diverge for 0≤ε<1/2; twin also at ε=1/2. Survivors [L]: signed size-cutoff endpoint including tail and primorial/fixed-finite-deletion endpoints uniformly bounded. The signed prime-divisor endpoint is uniformly bounded for every squarefree P (`hypercubeDilationDirichletForm_half_bounded`), via uniform local exponential integrability; no sub-half bound. Uniform natural head iff admission; full bounded cofinal size forms suffice. Needs: signed interior bound cofinally as ε↓0. No full-receiver divergence. E: V:TheHypercubeDirichletFormDivergesBelowHalfDamping; [PM endpoint sources](rh-current-frontier-map.md#ssa).
- Spectral prime-subset product budget in 0≤σ<1 — Excluded [L]: every nonnegative integrable positive-mass spectral weight gives unbounded finite-prime products. Arithmetic hyperbox divergence reaches −1/2≤ε<1/2. Survivor [L]: size-truncated form=full natural form=forward-dilation integral; exterior paid, ε=1/2 fully paid for μ. Needs: interior bound at arbitrarily small positive damping. Prime-subset divergence does not imply size-cutoff divergence. E: [PM spectral/receiver sources](rh-current-frontier-map.md#pm).
- Nonzero-mean bounded-pattern admission below half damping — Excluded [L, existing Cesàro mean L≠0, 0<ε<1/2]: own-mean-centered damped receiver satisfies `E_T/T^(1−2ε)→ΓεL²>0`, `Γε=[ζ(1−ε)/(1−ε)]²/(1−2ε)`. Survivors [L]: exact leading coefficient, finite-kernel pole-value law and mean-zero little-o. Actual μ/twin energy ratio→0 globally at each fixed ε; twin energy≥cεT^(1−2ε). A supplied summatory power saving lowers the positive growth exponent, not to boundedness. Needs: complete signed-energy bound. E: V:TheMeanValueIsTheLeadingCoefficientOfTheReceiver; V:TheParityPairAtTheLeadingExponent.
- Fixed-word/forcing-normalized rigidity — Nonselective [L, actual source, 0<ε<1/2]: F→∞ and Eμ/F→0 erase receiver scale; fixed-word displacement/work vanish relative to escape. Survivors [L]: finite forcing L¹ mass, bounded power-subtracted L² remainder, pole trace, localized/quantile antipodal laws, bounded dilation commutator, residual/trace reconstruction, exact trace-domain residual kernel=ℝqε with ℓε(qε)=1, bounded actual eta transform with retained floor-rounding, absolute word-tail≤`bKε(H+1)^−ε` and common countable covariance limit. - One Euler factor determines the pole trace — Refuted [L], 0<ε<1/2: `w(n)=n^(1−ε)cos(2πlog₂n)` has finite pronic energy after applying `Id−2^(1−ε)D₂`, but infinite energy after subtracting every cn^(1−ε). For each distinct zero, E(ρ)≤16(C+log(|γ|+4)); off-line E(ρ)≥1. Window energy≤16·#window·(C+log(|T|+H+4)) [L], not subquantum. Window full-row energy≥off-line count; RH iff every unit window has energy<2. For compact f, 4L²M_f≤E_f(L)≤8(cosh L−1)M_f and E_f(L)/L²→4M_f. At σ+iγ, σ>1, a zero β+iγ of multiplicity m forces prime cosine field≤archimedean field−m/(σ−β); this exterior estimate does not continue as an inside-strip sign. Finite-configuration energy is invariant under s↦½+λ(s−½)+iκ, λ≠0; field scales by λ^−1. Nonnegative twisted Selberg source selects prime sign phases, not the continued upper sign. At a flipped prime p, coefficient margin=2log²p; real σ>1 series margin≥2log²p·p^(−σ) [L], not a margin on arbitrary signed tests. Terminal-lens Harnack spreads the outward floor to half-radius discs with factor 1/3 [S]; no arithmetic upper bound. Discrete mean-square [L] and prime-detector power-saving counts [L] allow exceptional zeros. The latter require log R(log X+7)≤θV², 0<θ<1; no off-line-zero→alignment implication or pointwise sign follows. Needs: copy/distinct energy comparison and arithmetic upper control below the off-line quantum; force-zero + finite-energy receiver assumes both inputs. Reflection plane has s₁+s₂=1+2i Im s₁ and ξ(s₁)ξ(1−conj s₁)=|ξ(s₁)|². Survivors: exact factorization and boundary nonvanishing. No infinite theta convergence or general lattice classification. E: BGG:RamanujanTorsionStabilizersAndMonomialCollapse.

E: R:XiCriticalValueAccumulation; R:XiFiniteCriticalFactorRigidity; R:XiLogarithmicTractFactorRigidity.
- W1C unitary Euler factors — Kill [L]: exact principal-minor Euler factorization of a unitary matrix forces diagonal modulus1 and zero off-diagonal entries; no modulus-p^(−½) diagonal factors. Survivor [L]: diagonal-unitary classification; changed arithmetic scattering/domain remains open. E: R:UnitaryEulerFactorRigidity.
- WBF strong completion preserves compact trace — Kill [L]: finite genealogy compressions cost0; completed nonzero-fibre compact-ideal trace cost∞. Operators, adjoints and Grams converge strongly, but norm gap≥1; compact repair cannot remove it. Survivor [L]: exact finite actions and compact-perturbation invariance. Depth-uniform arithmetic completion still missing. E: H:PaperGenealogyTraceCompletion.
- TT positive displacement coefficients — Kill [L]: third-minor/slope degrees16/17; slope leading displacement coefficient −48·512·12^18·v^9(3k+1)^4(3k+4)<0 for v>0,k>−⅓ at every center; divided-difference obstruction retained. Survivor [L]: exact leading term and degree. No actual-source sign counterexample; bounded-domain/interior sign remains open. E: R:ThetaCusp/ThetaCuspRatioKineticLeading/SlopeLeading.

- Pointwise half-plane nonvanishing as Wiener invertibility — Refuted [L]: explicit three-term critical-Wiener kernel has no half-plane L-series zeros but is not a unit. Survivor [L]: unit iff a uniform positive lower modulus bound holds on the closed half-plane; inversion is governed by the full Bohr polydisk. Every tied radial Bohr character is approached by one vertical sequence on all Wiener series simultaneously; augmentation is approached as Re s→∞ [L]. Neither statement identifies the actual μ hull. Needs: uniform bound or genuine nonunit repair, not pointwise zero-freeness. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/NonvanishingOnTheHalfPlaneDoesNotMakeAUnit.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/AKernelBoundedBelowOnTheHalfPlaneIsAUnit.lean (`isUnit_iff_exists_le_norm_lSeries`). E: O:TiedBohrToriAreLimitsOfVerticalCharacters.
- Half-plane points as the entire repairing hull — Refuted [L, tower weights]: a dyadic tower pole forces a vertical progression and a full Bohr slice containing points not represented by any half-plane evaluation. Survivor [L]: hull scalar/conjugation laws, sum containment and convolution transport; finite probes have exact quotient codimensions. [L] Smooth and log-power sources share a hull but have different, respectively radical/nonradical repairing ideals; pole multiplicity is invisible to the point hull. Needs: actual μ ideal/hull control, not generic half-plane classification. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/ATowerPoleObstructsAVerticalProgression.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/ObstructionHullsAreNotConfinedToTheHalfPlane.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/TheCalculusOfTheObstructionHull.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/UnpayabilityCarriesMultiplicity.lean.
- Uniform lower Riesz bound for residue modes — Refuted [L]: residue synthesis has no uniform positive lower ℓ² bound. Survivor [L]: upper Bessel bound 48; actual μ half-damped residue sums converge in receiver norm despite divergent coefficient square sum. Half-damping chain has finite-input tail bound `C K^(−1/10)` and operator-norm continuity from above despite failure of Hilbert–Schmidt summability. Needs: signed source estimates below half damping, not coefficient norm equivalence. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheResidueModesAreBesselButNotRiesz.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheChainIsCompactAtHalfDamping.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/TheChainIsNormContinuousAtHalfDamping.lean.
- Undersampled companions as derivative payment — Refuted [L, polynomial/PW models]: two polynomial companions short never pay; one short pays only at a nodal-polynomial critical point. If Ah>π, a bandlimited source can vanish on the entire companion lattice with nonzero target derivative. Survivor [L]: sharp Nyquist price `A³h/(3π)`, perturbation-stable payment and exact configuration kernel-Gram ceiling. [L] At Ah=π, deleting one sample pays iff its derivative-kernel coefficient vanishes. Needs: actual arithmetic companion supply at adequate density and price. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/OneCompanionShortPaysOnlyAtCriticalPoints.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/PaleyWienerSourcesPayAtTheNyquistDensity.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/SignedArithmeticEnergyIncidence/AtTheNyquistDensityEveryCompanionCounts.lean.
- Single admitted damping as finite-curve purity — Refuted [L, finite-curve model]: two-eigenvalue damped chain is admitted despite an above-threshold outer eigenvalue. Survivor [L]: exact admission/zero-chain equivalence; below-threshold admitted dampings are finite for each nonpole eigenvalue, and accumulation forces the modulus bound. Needs: cofinal actual-source admission, not one tuned damping. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCompensationTheory/TheCurveReceiverIsAdmittedExactlyOnZeroChains.lean (`curveReceiver_admits_chainPair`, `norm_le_of_accPt_curveReceiverDampings`).
- Universal integrated polar domination for log-concave gauges — Refuted [L] exactly at moduli {2,3,4,5,7,11,13}; for log-concave M(x)/x, exceptional set {2,3,4,5,7,11}. Survivor [L]: exact complementary domination law; shifted θ gauges eventually pay outside {2,3,4,5,7}; exceptional integer-log interior crossing is (2,2), where reflected difference vanishes. Needs: arbitrary profile-weighted complete prime–pole payment, not an unweighted collar sign. E: I:TheIntegratedAtomPoleLawOfLogConcaveGauges; I:ShiftedGroundStateAndFirstJetAtomPoleLaw.
- Scalar comb energy as signed charge determination — Nonselective [L]: same mass M and comb energy E realize every charge in `[−√(ME),√(ME)]`. Survivor [L]: signed amplitude completes the reading; horizon-translate subtraction gives exact residual Cauchy–Schwarz control. Needs: test-uniform complete residual allocation, not scalar energy alone. E: Q:TheNumericalUnitForTheIncidenceLedger.

- Sparse-node blindness as pole control — Nonselective [L]: sub-square-root-density nodes allow any pole correction at arbitrarily small mass/archimedean cost; zero-pole/unrestricted blind infima agree for two towers and finite-prime smooth sets. Unrestricted all-integer-blind symbol is negative. Survivor: line-design enclosures and exact window Lévy tail floor; zero-pole prime-power threshold remains RH-equivalent. Needs: complete prime-power/polar sign, not extension of sparse-node invariance to all primes. E: B:BlindSymbolValueAndLineDesigns; B:PrimeBlindLevySlackAndWindowCone.

- PM separate absolute inversion control below half damping — Refuted [L]: actual inversion-defect pronic norm is unbounded for `0<ε≤1/2`; positive majorant admission iff ε>1/2. Survivor: complete admission iff block-energy admission iff sublinear bound on the signed inversion-minus-dilation pairing. Actual T=1 positive tangent autocorrelation coexists with negative damping work near zero; pairing positivity does not force relative stability. Needs: signed cross-damping cancellation, not separate absolute control. E: C:SingleDampingSublinearPairingAndBlockEnergy; C:SourceAngleShapeHierarchyAndIntegratedAutocorrelation.

- Odd polar neutrality as a finite-grid improvement — Nonselective [L]: symmetric distant copies cancel sinh moment, preserve bounded-window normalized correlations and approximate normalized Lévy energy. Survivor: odd-polar finite-grid lower-bound constants equal general odd constants; source wrapper gives an RH-sufficient odd-polar positivity target. Support expands; no all-prime blindness or complete-energy preservation. Needs: full signed prime–Gamma control, not a finite-grid gain. E: B:OddSectorPolarHyperplaneAndPoleWeightSynthesis.
- Source-defined sections as automatic RR — Nonselective [L]: degree-nonnegative ample E has RR iff `openPositiveCone⊆E` and full defect≥0; defining sections does not discharge the second condition. Actual Chebyshev cone equals the sign cone after source identification; golden model refutes universal Frobenius-mass RR/reflected Bézout. Survivors: exact floor classification and low-grid-blind RR+ample [L]; exact arithmetic-source graded/global RR [L], with ampleness/Bézout still RH-equivalent. Needs: arithmetic full-defect estimate. E: formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCastelnuovoSeveri/SectionEffectivityAndTheUniversalFloorLaw.lean; formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticOrientationTheory/AnEffectivityFromSectionsThatDoesNotReadTheForm.lean; G:ExactArithmeticSourceEffectivity; O:AllApertureChebyshevSourceEffectivity.
- ACS source reserves / fibre-linear degrees as automatic Bézout — Refuted [L]: mixed-defect domination is necessary; low-grid-blind half-coercivity has RR+ampleness but fails Bézout at every positive aperture. Survivors [L]: for nonnegative even fibre-invariant Φ, closed/strict RR+Bézout iff Φ=diagonal defect; graded version additionally forces Φ≥0. Without evenness [L], closed/strict RR+Bézout iff max(Φ,0)=diagonal defect, graded iff Φ=diagonal defect≥0. Closed/strict RR alone iff max(Φ,0)≤defect; graded RR iff Φ≤defect. Asymmetry/negative reserve supplies no loophole. Fibre-degree cone has Bézout iff primitive defect=0 [L]. Exact source representation is supplied [L]; its sign remains ACS/HF's obstacle. E: G:SourceConeBoundaryRigidity; G:GradedSourceConeBoundaryRigidity; G:SourceConeBoundaryApplications; G:SourceRiemannRochWithoutSymmetry.
- ACS finite readings / mass as Bézout — Refuted [L, every positive aperture]: arbitrary nonlinear function of finitely many linear readings AND exact L² mass cannot supply Bézout. C∞ unit-mass kernel witnesses have unbounded Weil energy. Every nonempty finite-cycle-reading preimage fails Bézout; arbitrarily small L² perturbations at fixed readings and both degrees have arbitrarily negative self-intersection. Survivors: exact arithmetic-source RR; full primitive-faithfulness requirement, ampleness iff nonpositive reserve lies in radical. Upper unboundedness is not negative Weil energy or failure of lower-floor routes. E: G:SmoothFiniteReadingKernels; G:FiniteReadingsAndMassCannotSupplyBezout; G:FiniteDegreeConesCannotSatisfyBezout; G:ExactSourceEffectivityRequiresInfinitelyManyDegrees; G:ExactArithmeticSourceEffectivity.
- ACS finite-degree effectivity sets as Bézout — Refuted [L]: for every finite-dimensional linear cycle reading L and arbitrary set A, Bézout on L⁻¹(A) iff L⁻¹(A) is empty, at every positive aperture and on the full scaling surface. Survivor [L]: self-intersection is unbounded below on each affine reading fibre, via a primitive kernel direction; neither self-intersection nor full pairing descends through a finite linear quotient. Stronger than a reserve-cone obstruction; not negative Weil energy or retirement of ACS. E: G:FiniteDegreeConesCannotSatisfyBezout.
- ACS positive majorant supplies RR automatically — Refuted [L, explicit arithmetic cones]: positive quadratic source reserves give Bézout+ampleness at every aperture and globally but fail RR. Finite Rosati golden cone likewise has Bézout+ampleness without unitarity/RR. Survivor: for Φ=defect+2R, R positive quadratic, Bézout iff defect+R≥0; graded RR iff R=0, closed RR iff R=0 and defect≥0. Global exponential source mass pays the complete prime sum. Needs: RR and sign for the SAME cone. E: G:BezoutCostsTwiceTheNegativeReserve; G:GlobalArithmeticMajorantsSupplyBezout; G:ExponentialSourceMassControlsTheFullPrimeSum; O:RosatiSectionsHaveBezoutWithoutUnitarity.
- Harmonic Euler positivity without pole control — Boundary [L, explicit models]: nonzero finite cosine polynomials with K≥1, P≥0 satisfy a₁<2a₀. Double origin pole permits boundary zeros with positive continuous Euler density; simple origin pole also permits a boundary zero when a second-harmonic pole is present. All-degree harmonic positivity alone excludes neither model. Survivor [L]: harmonic log-derivative positivity, divisor-order limits and degree-two coefficients (6,8,2) force quantized boundary zero order zero when origin pole order≤1 and harmonic poles absent; actual ζ boundary nonvanishing follows non-circularly. The module's interior collars are the imported `c/(log|t|)^9` region, not a consequence of the forcing law; dlVP, Littlewood and VK regions [L] add growth input to the same positivity, and the next graded rung is a fixed strip. Compact mixed-test formula retains all prime terms but gives only an upper bound on an unsigned off-line zero sum. Arithmetic survivor [L]: weighted-Λ cosine profile `2−2cos(t₀log n)` has boundary masses 2 at 0, −1 at ±t₀, zero elsewhere, attaining the progression bound D(0)/2; no global meromorphic Euler-product realization asserted. Arbitrary finite positive-height sets satisfy the sharp total bound Σ−D(z)≤D(0)/2 without commensurability [L]: [total zero mass](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/TotalZeroMass.lean). Capacity classification [L]: every integer-valued positive-definite D with D(0)=k and D≤0 off zero has finite form `kδ₀−Σm_z(δ_z+δ_−z)`, `2Σm_z≤k`; every allowed placement is realized by nonnegative twisted-Λ weights, not a proved completed Euler system. E: [BoundaryZeroCapacity](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/BoundaryZeroCapacity.lean). Open: interior forcing beyond growth input, not arbitrary harmonic-degree escalation. E: G:HarmonicCouplingForcesSourceBlindness; R:ZetaZeroFreeRegion; R:Sieve/VinogradovKorobovZeroFree; [weighted-prime realization](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/ArithmeticRealization.lean).
- Integer prime multiplicities as boundary-order integrality — Refuted [L, log-derivative weights]: multiplicities in {0,1,2} realize D(0)=1, D(1)=−1/2 and D≤0 off zero. Survivor: greedy rounding preserves every bounded nonnegative weighted-prime boundary profile, with cumulative error≤log(n+1); finite even profiles at arbitrary real heights are realizable by nonnegative weights or integer-multiplicity weights iff their Bohr polynomial is nonnegative. The source proves weight/profile identities, not a global meromorphic Euler-product realization. Needs: meromorphic divisor orders as a separate analytic input, then interior forcing; no actual-ζ or parent-route no-go. E: [FiniteBoundaryDivisors](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/FiniteBoundaryDivisors.lean); [IntegralRealization](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/IntegralRealization.lean). Lattice survivor [L]: shrinking rational-prime windows with integer multiplicities O((log p)²) realize every progression profile cos(au) on (2π/h)ℤ, h>0, including lattice and alternating branches. No global meromorphic completion asserted. E: [IntegralLatticeRealization](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/IntegralLatticeRealization.lean).
- Absolute pole-order budget from insertion/removal counts — Insufficient [L, count model]: `indexIncrement=poleIncrement+R_crit−I_crit`; counts (I_off,I_crit,R_off,R_crit)=(2,0,0,1) give 1<2 (`absolute_inequality_fails`). This is not an actual Weil-index theorem. Survivor [L]: exact conservation and index-identity-conditional count bound; refunded-quartet nonnegative density, transform antisymmetry and reflective factors. Completed realization [L]: `CompletedPositiveSystem 2 1` with nonreal off-critical quartet is constructed by dividing ξ by a Hardy critical-pair factor and inserting the quartic; removable singularities and all completion/positivity fields supplied. Needs: actual Weil-index identification, not completed-deformation existence. No index budget for ζ follows. E: R:WeilIndexConservation; R:RefundedComplexStray; R:WeilIndexBudgetBridge; [StripReflectionRigidity](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/StripReflectionRigidity.lean).
- Coupled height channels as an RH closure — Boundary scope [L, completed positive systems]: finite-height positive-ordinate collar multiplicity≤⌊κ/2⌋ for width≥cκ/[d log(3+max(T,0))], cκ>0, with no separation or low-ordinate cutoff. At κ=1 the whole collar |Im s|≤T is zero-free, including the real axis. No fixed strip follows. Survivors [L]: sharp half-pole mass law and boundary-twisted ζ with r boundary zeros at κ=2r+1; odd-κ capacity attained, even-κ attainment open. Needs: independent interior growth/sign; no universal no-go for coupled-height methods. E: [SharpHalfPoleCollar](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/SharpHalfPoleCollar.lean); [CollectivePoleRepulsion](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/CollectivePoleRepulsion.lean).
- Completed right-half-plane positivity + FE forces purity at κ=2 — Refuted [L]: degree-one `CompletedPositiveSystem 2 1` admits any real pair β,1−β, 0<β<1, β≠1/2, and nonreal off-critical quartets at depths 0<a≤1/4. Entire order-one completion, conjugation/reflection and positive-definite right log-derivative sections are constructed; no ζ/simple-pole counterexample. Survivors [L]: strip-reflection log-derivative identity; `4ord_(1/2)Λ+8Σ_(x∈X)ord_xΛ+5d<9κ`, finite X⊂(1/2,1]; κ=0 impossible, κ=1 forces d=1 and no real zeros. Entire positive-Laplace κ=0 impossibility also holds without an order bound. Needs: nonreal interior control at κ=1; actual Weil-index bridge remains separate. E: [StripReflectionRigidity](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/StripReflectionRigidity.lean). Pole-budget-one survivor [L]: every `CompletedPositiveSystem 1 d F Λ` has d=1, F=Kζ on Re s>1 and Λ=2Kξ globally, real K≠0; conversely these multiples are systems. Thus neither a distinct base system nor a zero-free completion exists inside this class; Hardy supplies arbitrarily high critical zeros. This identifies ζ, not its off-critical zero exclusion. E: [TheSimplePoleClassIsZeta](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/TheSimplePoleClassIsZeta.lean). Contraction survivors [L]: real pairs move to 1/2 at unchanged κ,d and finite iteration removes every real off-critical zero in a transformed system. Nonreal quartets at depth a contract at Δκ=1 for a≤1/4, Δκ=2 for a<1/2. These pole-paid constructions leave κ=1. Anchor-paid and height-coupled alternatives [L] below do not produce a contraction inside the original κ=1 class; a system carrying a double-anchored quartet has κ≥3 (below). No minimum-cost theorem for arbitrary rational multipliers. E: [SymmetricSquareContractionsAndExteriorCounts](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/SymmetricSquareContractionsAndExteriorCounts.lean).
- Isolated pure-ratio zero-tax quartet contraction — Obstruction [L]: `quartetCritPoly γ / quartetOffPoly a γ`, 0<a<1/2, γ≠0, does not have positive-definite vertical −log-derivative sections on every σ>1. Survivor [L]: finite conjugation-symmetric polar sums with such positivity have nonnegative Laplace scale weight; positive nonreal rightmost residue c forces real residue≥c at the same depth, ≥2c in the single-ordinate case. Exterior-square quartet divisor reduces to one real pair plus four critical zeros; for 0<a<1/4 the real pair admits a zero-boundary-pole midpoint multiplier. Anchor-paid survivor [L]: at depth a, two real anchors of depth b≥a, or one of depth b≥2a, with b<1/2, permit a completed-system contraction at unchanged κ,d. Height lift [L]: `Fγ=F²F(s+iγ)F(s−iγ)` constructs central/shifted pole orders (2κ,κ) and supplies double real anchors; contractions retain this enlarged data. Finite ordinate multiplicity N obeys `8N+4ord_(1/2)Λ+4ord_(1/2+iγ)Λ<18κ+3d log(3+|γ|)−7d/2`; real-zero elimination terminates in a transformed system. No actual ζ exterior lift, general pole-tax lower bound or original κ=1 contraction. Coupled-rigidity implication [L]: scalar proportionality would exclude an off-line quartet via its +4 central-order increment. Universal scalar rigidity is refuted by critical splitting [L]. All-ordinate rational/positive central ceilings are each equivalent to RH [L]; neither ceiling is established. This does not identify the unrestricted HeightCoupledSystem ceiling with those subclasses. E: PL:RigidityOfTheCoupledClassExcludesItsZeros; BGG:ChiralDeformationAndNonRigidityOfHeightCoupledSystems [L]. E: [ExteriorAndHeightCoupledQuartetContractions](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/ExteriorAndHeightCoupledQuartetContractions.lean).
- Universal scalar rigidity of HeightCoupledSystem 2 1 1 γ — Refuted [L]: critical splitting gives non-proportional positive deformation at every critical-zero ordinate. Positive prime-mass rigidity at γ iff no ξ zero has ordinate γ, and rational deformations are rigid at such γ; two-sided scalar rigidity of the full class fails at every ξ-zero ordinate and is not proved at zero-free ordinates. Positive chirality at γ iff no off-critical zero has ordinate γ. All-ordinate chirality and rational/positive central ceilings each iff RH. Under horizontal off-critical exclusion, divisor jump is even positive-definite and bounded by central drop; Abel-negligible boundary error suffices. Under the same exclusion and no central drop, deficit abscissa≤1/2 and (σ−1/2)D(σ)→0 force coefficient equality/scalar proportionality; strict sub-half deficit is sufficient. Different coefficients force failure of zero residue, not existence of a positive residue limit. Central excess forces deficit abscissa≥1/2 and liminf_(σ↓1/2)(σ−1/2)D(σ)≥excess; no finite residue limit asserted. Survivors [L], still under horizontal exclusion: zero-residue half-power deficit makes the surplus Abel limit the even central drop D≤2ord_(1/2+iγ)ξ; finitely supported critical jumps obey r(0)+2Σr(t)cos(θt)≥0 for every θ. An assumed full-class central ceiling transfers to entire cofactors of (2ξ)^k; this is not an automorphic construction or identification with the rational/positive subclasses. Needs: actual all-ordinate ceiling/chirality or arithmetic sign; horizontal exclusion cannot discharge itself. E: BGG:ChiralDeformationAndNonRigidityOfHeightCoupledSystems; BGG:PositiveCoupledRigidityAndTheCriticalTransferLaw; BGG:TheAbelGramLawOfPositiveDeformations; BGG:StrongMultiplicityOneBelowTheSquareRootDeficit; BGG:CentralExcessForcesASquareRootPrimeDeficit; BGG:TheDeficitResidueBoundsTheCentralExcess; BGG:TheCriticalConservationLaw; BGG:TheQuantizationOfTheSquareRootSurplus; BGG:TheCentralCeilingTransfersToFactors.
- Degree-one/isobaric local exterior shortcut — Scope boundary [L]: local degree-one second exterior trace=0; for g_m(θ)=m+2cosθ, m∈ℕ, the second exterior polynomial is `(m²−m+2)/2+2mcosθ`, negative at π for 1≤m≤4 and independent of θ at m=0. Survivor [L]: strict positivity for every m≥5, with constant/half-harmonic ratio≥11/5. Needs: actual ζ exterior carrier and arithmetic count positivity; these local identities do not prohibit a different global lift. E: [IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost.lean).
- Cost-free shifted-pole absorption by additive positive-definite boundary twists — Excluded within class [L]: D even positive-definite, D(u)≤0 for u≠0, γ≠0, D(γ)≤−m imply D(0)≥2m. Survivor [L]: `HeightCoupledSystem (κ₀+2κγ) 0 d γ` with unchanged completion, produced by `boundaryTwistFactor γ^κγ`; original height lift becomes central order 4κ. Keeping shifted poles gives exact coefficient bγ=4/(4+γ²)+2/(1+γ²), while absorption costs 3−bγ at s=2. Needs: control coupled Gamma/complex zeros; absorption does not return to `CompletedPositiveSystem 1 d`, and no lower bound for arbitrary non-additive lifts follows. E: [IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost.lean). Fejér survivor [L]: degree-K lift, exact multiplicities and anchor-paid contractions yield central pole coefficient `9(K+1)/(2K)→9/2`, with all shifted-pole and Gamma sums retained. The shifted Gamma pair at 2 lies between `log(3+|γ|)−5` and `log(3+|γ|)+1/2`; the full budget is not its central coefficient. Nonnegative cosine multipliers with first coefficient≥2 require constant≥sec(π/(K+2))>1. No original-κ=1 quartet exclusion. [ACS frontier](rh-current-frontier-map.md#acs).
- Unshifted double-anchored quartet at κ≤2 — Excluded within class [L]: `CompletedPositiveSystem κ d`, 0<a<1/2, γ≠0, real order at 1/2+a≥2 and Λ(1/2+a+iγ)=0 imply κ≥3. Survivor [L]: for matched multiplicities M across finitely many depths, `[16+144/(9+4γ²)]M+4ord_(1/2)Λ+72ord_(1/2+iγ)Λ/(9+4γ²)+5d<9κ`; R_a² plants two real-pair copies at Δκ=2, preserving d and other zero orders. That planting cost is minimal for finite positive-definite polar-sum multipliers with nonpositive residues off {0,1}. Needs: original-ζ exclusion without absent anchors; κ≥3 is not κ=3 attainment or a lower bound for every complex contraction. E: [IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/ArithmeticCorrespondencePositivity/IntrinsicExteriorAndMinimumAnchorCost.lean).
- Infinite normalized gauge evades finite pole capacity — Excluded [L, specified gauge class]: bounded G on a right half-plane, G(σ)→1, entire finite-order completion `E=(s(s−1))^κG` and `E(1−s)=εE(s)`, |ε|=1, imply ε=1 and monic E of degree 2κ. Survivor: total zero multiplicity≤2κ; conjugation-symmetric distinct-positive-ordinate quartets obey `2Σm≤κ`; exponential Laplace gauges with finite absolute exponential moment satisfy the boundedness/normalization conditions. Not a classification of arbitrary completed Euler systems. E: [InfiniteGaugePoleRigidity](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/InfiniteGaugePoleRigidity.lean).
- Nontrivial entire ξ deformation with positive relative Laplace correction — Excluded [L]: nonzero entire reflected E, nonvanishing on a right ray, with `−E′/E+ξ′/ξ=Laplace(ν)` there, ν≥0 and one finite exponential moment, forces ν|(0,∞)=0 and E=cξ. Survivor: exact rigidity without finite-order growth; Landau abscissa obstruction and reflection remove the correction. Source identification preserves ξ's unknown zero location; no RH closure or exclusion of signed/pole-changing deformations. E: [refund rigidity](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/StripBochnerWeilForcing/InfiniteGaugePoleRigidity.lean).
- Signed-weight simple-pole exclusion at deficit ε≥1 — Refuted [L, boundary-weight model]: weights `Λ(n)n^(−1−x)(1−2cos(log n))≥−Λ(n)n^(−1−x)` realize `δ₀−δ₁−δ_−1`. Survivor [L]: for integer D with D(0)=k, D≤0 off zero and absolutely summable signed boundary weights bounded below by −ε times ζ weights, exact capacity is `2Σm≤k+ε`; universal simple-pole exclusion iff ε<1. Needs: actual arithmetic signed-margin control or interior forcing. Not a completed Euler-system counterexample or route retirement. E: [PositivityMargin](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/PositivityMargin.lean). Square-budget survivor [L]: for bounded signed g with normalized signed/square boundary limits, `D(0)²+2Σ_(u∈U)D(u)²≤R`; D(0)=R=1 excludes every off-origin boundary value. The exceptional g=1−2cos(log n) instead has R=3, attaining equality. Trace limits, not an actual completed Rankin–Selberg construction. E: [RankinSelbergBessel](../../formal/GaloisForLFunctions/MultiplicativePositivityGeometry/RankinSelbergBessel.lean).
- Positive definiteness alone as boundary realizability — Refuted [L]: `1_ℚ` is positive definite on discrete ℝ but has no nonnegative summable boundary-weight realization. Exact range=sequential pointwise closure of positive cosine polynomials. Locally finite survivor [L]: even positive definiteness iff realization by one fixed nonnegative profile on rational-prime-power logs, `Λ(n)g(log n)n^(−1−x)`; no freely chosen frequencies required. Integer-prime-exponent extensions: locally finite range via greedy rounding [L]; every absolutely summable even positive-definite profile via bounded Bohr profile and rounding [L]. Countable sums with summable origin mass preserve realizability; a weighted lattice sum has dense support exactly 2πℚ [L]. Thus general boundary support need not be locally finite. - Finite cosine forcing overcomes its supplied growth payment at arbitrary single-target width — Excluded [L, this scalar comparison]: for L>0, growth payment L·S with S≥a₁, degree K buys only `η<(1−1/√(2cos(π/(K+2))))²/L`; degree one buys none. Restricting δ≤δ₁ retains the criterion when Aη≤Gδ₁². For frequencies ω_j=Σ_i n_ji g_i, ℚ-independent g_i, n_ji∈ℕ, 1≤Σ_i n_ji≤M, signed Σa_j≤Ma₀ regardless of generator count; one-generator Fejér packets attain Ma₀ [S]. For mixed-sign integer vectors n_j≠0, weights m with 1≤|n_j·m|≤M yield the same signed bound; such weights exist for every fixed finite cloud [S]. Survivors [L]: positive convolution ideals without sharp are half-blind; the positive left-blind ideal equals bounded-backward-flow sources, strictly contains the full blind ideal if ¬RH, and is the whole source iff RH. - Diagonal/ratio/product couplings select the real arithmetic gauge — Blind [L/S, finite positive-order kernels]: actual prime kernel P^ε(m,n)=ε(m)ε(n)P(m,n), Q^ε(x)=Q(εx), with preserved ℓ² mass; every real sign twin, including Liouville, shares all finite prime-form spectral ceilings/floors and global unboundedness. At w≤c₀log(1+1/N), c₀>0 independent of N, every such twin has M=arch−prime≥0 for arbitrary real coefficients [L]. This is not equality on fixed tests: below atom spacing, nonnegative weights give M_ω≥M; on {1,p} with ω(p)=−1, M_ω−M=(2log p/√p)‖f‖₂² for every N≥p [L]. ζ is the minimum on this cone; the margin cannot select it as uniquely positive. On gauged tests, M_ω(f_x)−M(f_(ωx)) is exactly A(f⁺+f⁻)−A(f⁺−f⁻), not zero [L]. General all-source coupling covariance iff nab is square on supported (n;a,b) [S]. For every real sign gauge flipping 2, 162(K+1)^4≤X gives ≥K charged cells and defect≥2K uniformly in c [L]; the Liouville-specific square/twice-square argument gives ≥J cells below 8J⁴ [S]. No formalized positive-density count (for Liouville, logarithmic density 1/2 is Tao's two-point logarithmic Chowla theorem, not formalized) or complete signed-form transfer. Full-form survivor [L, fixed fraction of spacing]: Re W=M+2C²−2S², hence Re W+2S²≥0 and Re W≥0 on |S|≤|C|; this cone is not the kernel S=0. Diamagnetic survivor [L]: the Lévy extension of M satisfies M(|f|)≤M_ω(f) for real compact C² f and any real |ω(n)|≤1; for nonnegative disjoint bumps, |f_x|=f_|x|. Every all-coefficient Markov lower bound on that family passes to all bounded twists. Universal spacing survivor [L]: for any real C² bump, without nonnegativity, and every real |ω|≤1, without multiplicativity, the same quantitative Markov floor holds at w≤c₁δ_N and nonnegativity at w≤c₀δ_N. This separated-regime bound uses coefficient sizes, not arithmetic selection; it is not full Weil positivity. Coupled-kernel survivor [S]: for completely multiplicative unimodular χ and N≥6,t≥2, A+tχ̄(m)χ(n)P has a nonnegative unit-phase gauge iff χ(p)=1 at all primes≤N. For the n^(iτ)-conjugated archimedean kernel, the criterion becomes χ(p)=p^(iτ) for primes≤N [S]. Successor edges plus (1,6) fix the gauge; this is not the full Weil form. Non-ratio survivor [L]: arbitrary-width correlation formulas locate coefficient-gauge defects at non-ratio prime lags; the infinite-sum identity retains summability premises. For N≥3 there are m<n≤N, n≠2m within 4δ_N of lag log2 [L]. Lag proximity alone supplies no nonzero overlap or signed margin; diamagnetism still holds. Full-pole survivor [L]: weighted reserve pays both poles on all independent-profile sections at a smaller fixed spacing fraction; no all-test or twin-selective sign follows. Successor monotonicity also fails [S, raw prime form]: δ₁+δ₃→δ₂+δ₄ lowers it, δ₂+δ₅→δ₃+δ₆ raises it. Survivor [S]: shifted-kernel defect identity; in the prime-Euler gauge the successor archimedean phase is conj(succConn), and nonnegative successor edges iff prime phases through N+1 equal 1. Neither is a full-Weil sign theorem. Needs: full signed polar control or a non-modulus-closed test-space estimate; gauge fixing and modulus-closed Markov floors alone are not RH. Separately RR holds for h≡1 and Bézout for h≡0; making both x and −x effective at x²>0 violates Bézout. Survivor: exact section-count criterion and conditional positive-cone indicator construction. Survivor: abundance iff degree-zero positive square; RI forces every rate c<x²/2 eventually on positive squares. Actual scaling-surface specialization iff RH. The nonnegative model h=½max(0,χ) has eventual paired growth≤(max(0,x²/2)+ε)n² for each ε>0; no exact endpoint bound or minimum over unrestricted signed h asserted. E: AE:TheEffectivityCalculus. Tate survivor [L]: approximate doubling gives a unique degree-two homogeneous limit with |q−h|≤C/3; bounded-below or midpoint-convex h gives q≥0. Real orbit positivity iff integral orbit positivity iff existence of a uniformly close bounded-below height iff existence of a uniformly close midpoint-convex height; every-cycle ζ specialization iff RH. Bounds may depend on cycle, not correspondence. Arithmetic height construction/identification remains open. E: AE:TatesLimitLaw; AE:TheConvexityOfCounts.
- Theta h⁰(degree) supplies surface Riemann–Roch — Excluded [L]: positivity, linear RR, bounded degree levels and negative-degree decay do not supply quadratic RI on any polar surface. RI forces h(nA)+h(κ−nA)≥cn² eventually for every c<1. Survivor [L]: shifted/modulated theta log-count has Tate limit πJ²/t, not an identified arithmetic Rosati trace. Finite positive Jacobi mixtures have h=q+O(1), q=ΣwπJ²/t; degree-zero subquadratic growth iff every channel vanishes there; otherwise quadratic inflation [L]. Unsigned large-sieve majorants force q≥½|self-pairing| and cannot deliver isotropic subquadratic growth [L]. ### Theta, Schur, spectral and positivity mechanisms (71) — constrains TH, RN, TG, WL, RW, SZ, LA

- TH linear Hilbert norm-square realization of native quartic theta charge — Kill [L, original degree-one source coordinates]: degree-four scaling contradicts degree-two scaling of every linear-map norm square. Survivor [L]: exact nonlinear two-coordinate feature `‖F(a,b)‖²=sourcePairPositiveQuarticCharge(a,b)`, summing over actual theta Schur modes. Open: source-derived all-degree theta moment sign; finite feature positivity is not that sign. E: R:ThetaQuarticHilbertReceiverBoundary.
- no-go-489 Strict full-theta outgoing Cauchy reserve. Kill [L]: for complete theta kernel `K`, every real tail and `c>0` admit a finite negative packet of `K−c·Φ(x)Φ(y)/(e^{2x}+e^{2y})`; no uniform-rank positive Cauchy margin. Survivor [L]: exact incoming diagonal law `K(−r,−r)/(r e^{-2r}Φ(−r)²)→1/(2π)` and sharp rank-one Cauchy projection. Sharp zero-reserve PSD remains open. E: R:ThetaTailCauchyCoercivityObstruction.
- no-go-490 Reflection-compatible full-theta Cauchy reserve. Kill [L]: with `q=e^{2x}+e^{-2x}`, every real tail and `c>0` admit a finite negative packet of `K−c·Φ(x)Φ(y)/(q(x)+q(y))`. Survivor [L]: charged-cusp `|Φ(z_N)|≥A N⁵`, `J(z_N,z_N)≤D N¹⁰`, analytic boundary admissibility; `J(z_N,z_N)/|Φ(z_N)|²→0` (also weighted horizontal `L²`) forces every valid comparison rate to diverge and every dominated analytic scalar feature `f` to satisfy `f(z_N)/Φ(z_N)→0`. Unsubtracted `K` sign open. E: R:ThetaTailReflectedCauchyObstruction; R:ThetaTailReflectedBoundaryObstruction; R:ThetaTailChargedEnergyCollapse.
- no-go-491 Uniform Hardy real-zero law across theta-power deformations. Kill [L]: `F_a=θ₃h^{a/4}` has only finitely many transform zeros in `|Im w|≤½` for `a>½` and infinitely many outside; exact modular inversion does not preserve infinitely many real zeros across all `a`. Survivor [L]: infinitely many real zeros for `0<a<½`; every transcendental `a∈(0,½)` has infinitely many off-line zeros; affected parameters form an open set whose complement in `(0,½)` is closed there, countable and algebraic. `a=½` gives completed `L(s,χ₈)` and `a=⅓` gives completed `L(s,χ₁₂)` up to nonzero factors; off-line zeros at `a=⅓` would imply ¬GRH(χ₁₂). Algebraic exceptions and Xi zero confinement open. E: R:ThetaPowerHardyZeros; R:ThetaPowerStripRigidity; R:ThetaPowerZerosOffCriticalStrip; R:ThetaPowerChiEightLFunction; R:ThetaPowerChiTwelveLFunction; R:ThetaPowerTranscendentalOffLineZero; R:ThetaPowerOffLineParameterSet; P1:theta-power-family-hardy-threshold; P3:theta-power-transcendental-offline-zeros.
- no-go-492 Fixed positive geometric reserve for complete theta kernel. Kill [L]: for every real tail and `ε>0`, some finite packet makes `K−εG` negative, `G(x,y)=∫₀∞√((x+s)(y+s))Φ(x+s)Φ(y+s)ds` on positive nodes; no fixed positive geometric reserve. Survivor [L]: exact logarithmic tangent finite part with coefficient `Φ(0)²/4`; negative dimensions of `K` on any real tail equal those of its double-Laplace Bezoutian on every `Re w=σ>0`. - Separate Lorentzian blocks or a positive shifted basis force the arithmetic Hodge sign — Invalid [S, finite quadratic forms]: two Lorentzian rank-one coordinate squares sum to positive-definite I₂. A large shift by H+V makes any finite real Gram with hyperbolic pole plane entrywise nonnegative without changing signature. Survivors [S]: common fixed-hyperplane nonpositivity is closed under nonnegative sums; Hermitian reverse Cauchy–Schwarz ⇔ positive index≤1, equivalent to RH on all actual scaling-surface Grams. For symmetric Q with D=Q1>0, Q[v]≤Σ_i(Qv)_i²/D_i iff hyperbolic links T_i sum to Q and satisfy T_i1=Qe_i; links can be constructed from rank-one columns plus negative-semidefinite residual. Connected support and nonnegative off-diagonal entries make this equivalent to hyperbolicity. Both conditions matter: I₂ satisfies the link inequality but is disconnected; [[2,−1],[−1,2]] satisfies it with connected support but is not Metzler. Each has two positive squares. Weighted survivor [S], symmetric Metzler Q and π>0: Qπ≤0 implies Q≤0; for Qπ>0 and connected support, hyperbolicity iff Q[v]≤Σ_iπ_i(Qv)_i²/(Qπ)_i. Frobenius calibration [S]: Gram(H,V,Γ₀,…,Γ_K) has connected support and an explicit π>0 with Qπ>0 for every curve datum; Metzler iff counts N₁,…,N_K≥0. The golden q=5 datum satisfies these conditions but fails TD_π and Hodge at K=1. Survivor: exact hyperbolic-pole split Q[(p,d)]=2(p_H+d·V)(p_V+d·H)−δ(d); Hodge iff δ≥0, equivalently TD_π under the stated count condition. Unitary data satisfy TD_π using unitarity as input; no ζ-source TD or links supplied. Polar-gluing survivor [S]: for a finite nonempty symmetric family with common positive anchor e, a_i=Q_i[e], b_i=Q_i(e,·), A=Σa_i, b=Σb_i, local reserves R_i=b_i²/a_i−Q_i obey R_sum=ΣR_i−V, V=Σa_i(b_i/a_i−b/A)²≥0. The sum is hyperbolic iff V≤ΣR_i. Subtracting the PSD matrix of V preserves the anchor row and adds reserves exactly, yielding a hyperbolic constructed form from hyperbolic inputs. The correction decomposes exactly into within-group plus between-group corrections; corrected gluing is associative as a quadratic form. Correction and gluing commute with linear pullback with mapped anchor. With all local reserves zero, the sum is hyperbolic iff normalized polar rows align. Sharp calibration [S]: opposite tilted planes [[1,±t],[±t,t²−r]] are individually hyperbolic iff r≥0, their sum iff t²≤r, correction diag(0,2t²). Quantitative survivor [S]: TD with error εD and an independent primitive spectral bound below μ₊=(1+√(1−4ε))/2 gives primitive bound μ₋D, 4ε≤1; no actual ζ TD or gap follows. Local-factor boundary [S]: the zero-diagonal prime kernel on 1,p,…,p⁵ has two positive squares for every prime p. Survivors: symmetric Metzler A has A−diag(A1)≤0; nonnegative finite local vectors have Lorentzian second-symmetric form (ΣM)(ΣM)ᵀ−ΣMMᵀ, and their finite-product Hessians are Lorentzian at positive local factors. This universal closure neither identifies the full Weil form nor selects ζ; no infinite-product transfer follows. Needs: actual ζ decomposition paying V, source identification/domination of the corrected form, shared-hyperplane sign or arithmetic links; constructing a corrected form does not prove the original form has the same signature. This does not exclude all arithmetic maps or full-Weil constructions. For n≥#spectrum, rank C=#distinct outer eigenvalues is minimal among all corrections yielding PSD; G+tC≥0 iff unitary or t≥1. Completion [[P,B],[Bᴴ,BᴴPB−G]] has positive index 1+negative index(G); the correction preserves P, both prescribed pole couplings B/Bᴴ and the diagonal-graph row, while subtracting C from the graph block. The same sharp threshold holds for every B. For real graph coefficients, repair energy is half the reflection defect in normalized coordinates. This repairs a different form; no uncorrected ζ sign follows. Needs: source-faithful conformal replacement or payment of the correction in the original arithmetic form.
